## Card conflicts

## Proposed: rows without finish or language

`riftbound_deck_cards`, the wishlist, and deck legend and champion printings store a printing but no finish or language.
**Proposed rule, for review, not applied:** EN, `normal` where it maps, else the only finish that maps (foil). The candidates per old printing are in `bot/riftbound-printing-map.json` (`without_finish_or_language`).

- foil: 714
- no proposal: 57
- normal: 637
